	});

	//save nfWB to localStorage
	var updateLocalStoreWB = function(){
		try {
		  localStorage.setItem('wbState', JSON.stringify(nfWB));
		} catch (e) {
		  if (e == QUOTA_EXCEEDED_ERR) {
		   console.log('Limit is exceeded');
		  }
		}
	}

	//make sidebar elements sortable
	sidebar.sortable({
    handle: '.wbs-cat-drag',
    update: updateOrder
  });
  

  //init exercises slider
  function initSlider(parent) {
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-images').slick({
	  	fade: true,
	  	dots: true,
	  	infinite: false,
	  	prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			appendArrows: parent+' .media-img-nav',
			appendDots: parent+' .media-img-nav'
		});
		$(parent+' .nfcf-videos').slick({
	  	fade: true,
	  	dots: true,
	  	infinite: false,
	  	prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			appendArrows: parent+' .media-video-nav',
			appendDots: parent+' .media-video-nav'
		}).on('beforeChange', function(e, slick, currentIndex) {
			var currentSlide = jQuery(slick.$slides[currentIndex]);
			var currVideo = currentSlide.find('video');
			if (currVideo.length) {
				currVideo[0].pause();
			}
		})
  }
  
  //destroy exercises slider
  function destroySlider(parent) {
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-images').slick('unslick');
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-videos').slick('unslick');
  }

  function clearExModalDescr(){
  	if (mobileSlider) {
  		destroySlider(mobileSlider)
  	}
  	exModalContent.html('');
  }

	//change video type
	body.on('click', '.btn-media-type', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
			var p = $(this).parents('div.nf-exercise-slider-wrap');
			p.toggleClass('nf-show-video');
			p.find('a.btn-media-type').toggleClass('active');
			if ($(this).hasClass('btn-img-type')) {
				p.find('video').each(function(){
					this.pause()
				})
			}
		}
	});
	body.on('keyup', '.btn-media-type, .slick-arrow', function(e){
		var code = (e.keyCode ? e.keyCode : e.which);
		if(code == 13) { //Enter keycode
		  $(this).click();
		}
	})

	//show exercise descr event
	body.on('click', '.wbs-exercise-link', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		p = $(this).parent();
		if (!p.hasClass('active')) {
			tempIds = $(this).attr('href').split('_');
			$('.wbs-exercise.active').removeClass('active');
			p.addClass('active');
			updateExDescr(tempIds[0],tempIds[1]);
		};
		if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
			openExInfoModal(tempIds[0],tempIds[1]);
			mobileSlider = '#ex-info-modal';
			initSlider(mobileSlider);
		}
		$(this).blur();
	})

	//exercise info link event
	body.on('click', '.ex-info', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		idArr = $(this).attr('href').split('_');
		openExInfoModal(idArr[0],idArr[1]);
		mobileSlider = '#ex-info-modal';
		initSlider(mobileSlider);
	});

	//category minus event
	body.on('click', '.wbs-cat-minus', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		var removeCat = confirm('Are you sure you want to remove this category?');
		if (removeCat) {
			var id = $(this).attr('href');
			clearCat(id);
			setActiveEx();
			if (!$('.wbs-cat-wrap').length) {
				wbWrap.removeClass('show-wb-app');
			}
			updateOrder();
		}
	});

	//category plus event
	body.on('click', '.wbs-cat-plus', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		id = +$(this).attr('href');
		exListWrap.html(createExList(nfWB.categories[id].exercises, id));
		paginateList();
		addToExistingCat = true;
		openExModal()
	})

	body.on('click', '.wbs-cat-title', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
	});

	//exercise minus event
	body.on('click', '.wbse-minus', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		if (confirm('Are you sure you want to remove this exercise?')) {
			idArr = $(this).attr('href').split('_');
			nfWB.categories[idArr[0]].exercises[idArr[1]].selected = false;
			$(this).parents('.wbs-exercise').remove();
			$('.wbs-cat-wrap[data-id="'+idArr[0]+'"]').removeClass('full-exs');
			setActiveEx();
			updateOrder();
		}
	});

	//exercise settings
	body.on('click', '.wbse-edit', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		idArr = $(this).attr('href').split('_');
		updateExSettings(idArr[0],idArr[1]);
		$.fancybox.open({
			src  : '#ex-settings-modal',
			type : 'inline',
			opts : {
				afterClose : function() {
					clearSettingsSlider()
				}
			}
		},{
			video: {
				autoStart: false
			}
		});
	});

	function updateExSettings(catId,exId) {
		clearSettingsValues()
  	settingsSlider = '#ex-settings-modal';
  	esSettingsSliderWrap.html(createWBSlider(nfWB.categories[catId].exercises[exId]))
  	initSlider(settingsSlider);
  	form = nfWB.categories[catId].exercises[exId].form;
  	esSettingsFormWrap.removeClass(
  		'wb-show-reps wb-show-reps-inp wb-show-reps-distance wb-reps-has-distance wb-show-time wb-show-each'
  	);
  	for (var p in form) {
  		updateForm(p, form)
  	}
	}

	function updateForm(field, form) {
		switch (field) {

		  case 'reps':
		  	if (esSettingsFormWrap.hasClass('wb-show-reps')) {
		  		esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-reps-has-distance wb-show-reps-inp');
		  	} else {
		  		esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-show-reps wb-show-reps-inp');
		  	}
		    repsI.val(form[field])
		    break;

		  case 'distance':
		    if (esSettingsFormWrap.hasClass('wb-show-reps')) {
		  		esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-reps-has-distance wb-show-reps-distance');
		  	} else {
		  		esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-show-reps wb-show-reps-distance');
		  	}
		  	distanceI.val(form[field].value);
		  	rebuildSelect(dimensionS, form[field].dimensions)
		    break;

		  case 'time':
		    esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-show-time');
		    timeDimention.text(form[field].dimension);
		    minTimeS.attr('data-short-dimention', form[field].dimensionShort)
		    rebuildSelect(minTimeS, form[field].min);
		    rebuildSelect(maxTimeS, form[field].max);
		    updMaxTimeSelect();
				updMinTimeSelect();
		    break;

		  case 'each':
		    esSettingsFormWrap.addClass('wb-show-each');
		    eachTrueS.prop('checked', form[field])
		    eachFalseS.prop('checked', !form[field])
		    break;

		  default:
		    break;
		}
	}

	//save exercise settings
	$('.wb-settings-done').click(function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		//idArr is actual
		form = nfWB.categories[idArr[0]].exercises[idArr[1]].form;
		for (var p in form) {
  		saveFormSettings(p, form);
  	}
  	saveRepsVal();
  	updateLocalStoreWB();
  	$.fancybox.close();
	});
	
	function saveFormSettings(field, form) {
		switch (field) {

		  case 'reps':
		    exRep = repsI.val();
		  	form[field] = exRep;
		    break;

		  case 'distance':
		    exDistance = distanceI.val() + ' ' + $("#dimension option:selected").text();
		  	form[field].value = distanceI.val();
		  	form[field].dimensions = [];
		  	saveSelectOpts(form[field].dimensions, '#dimension');
		    break;

		  case 'time':
		    exTime = $("#min-time option:selected").val();
		    exTime += $("#max-time option:selected").val() === 'none' ? '' : ' - ' + $("#max-time option:selected").val();
		    exTime += form[field].dimensionShort;
		    form[field].min = [];
		    form[field].max = [];
		  	saveSelectOpts(form[field].min, '#min-time');
		  	saveSelectOpts(form[field].max, '#max-time');
		    break;

		  case 'each':
		    exEach = eachTrueS.prop('checked') ? 'ea' : false;
		  	form[field] = eachTrueS.prop('checked');
		    break;

		  default:
		    break;
		}
	}
	
	function saveRepsVal() {
		repVal = exRep ? exRep : '';
		if ( exEach && (exDistance || exTime) ) {
			exEach = ' ea';
		}
		if (exTime) {
			repVal += exRep ? ' ' + exTime : exTime
		}
		if (exDistance) {
			repVal += repVal ? ' ' + exDistance : exDistance;
		}
	  if (exEach) {
	  	repVal += exEach;
	  }
		$('.wbse-reps[data-id="'+idArr[0]+'_'+idArr[1]+'"]').text(repVal);
		nfWB.categories[idArr[0]].exercises[idArr[1]].repsVal = repVal;
		//refresh tempValues
		exRep = false;
		exDistance = false;
		exTime = false;
		exEach = false;
	}

	function saveSelectOpts(arr, select) {
		Array.from(document.querySelector(select).options).forEach(function(opt) {
		  arr.push(
		   {
		   	value: opt.value,
		   	text: opt.text,
		   	selected: opt.selected
		   }
		  )
		});
	}

	function rebuildSelect(s, optsArr) { // s - select selector
		s.empty();
  	var options = '', items = '', parent = s.parent();
  	optsArr.forEach(function(e){
  		if (e.selected) {
  			parent.find('.select-selected').text(e.text);
  			items += '<div data-val="'+e.value+'" tabindex="0" class="same-as-selected">'+e.text+'</div>';
  			options += '<option selected value="'+e.value+'">'+e.text+'</option>';
  		} else {
  			items += '<div data-val="'+e.value+'" tabindex="0">'+e.text+'</div>';
  			options += '<option value="'+e.value+'">'+e.text+'</option>';
  		}
  	});
  	parent.find('.select-items').html(items);
  	s.append(options);
  	parent.find('.select-items>div').each(function(){
  		this.addEventListener("click", function(e) {
	        var y, i, k, s, h, sl, yl;
	        s = this.parentNode.parentNode.getElementsByTagName("select")[0];
	        sl = s.length;
	        h = this.parentNode.previousSibling;
	        if (!$(this).hasClass('same-as-selected')) {
	        	for (i = 0; i < sl; i++) {
		          if (s.options[i].innerHTML == this.innerHTML) {
		            s.selectedIndex = i;
		            h.innerHTML = this.innerHTML;
		            y = this.parentNode.getElementsByClassName("same-as-selected");
		            yl = y.length;
		            for (k = 0; k < yl; k++) {
		              y[k].removeAttribute("class");
		            }
		            this.setAttribute("class", "same-as-selected");
		            break;
		          }
		        }
		        if ($(s).attr('id') === 'min-time') {
		        	updMaxTimeSelect()
		        }
		        if ($(s).attr('id') === 'max-time') {
		        	updMinTimeSelect()
		        }
	        }
	        h.click();
	    });
  	})
  	
	}

	function clearSettingsValues() {
		exRep = false;
		exDistance = false;
		exTime = false;
		exEach = false;
	}

	function clearSettingsSlider() {
		if (settingsSlider) {
  		destroySlider(settingsSlider)
  	}
  	esSettingsSliderWrap.html('');
	}

	function clearCat(id) {
		nfWB.categories[id].selected = false;
		nfWB.categories[id].exercises.forEach(function(e){
			e.selected = false;
		});
		$('.wbs-cat-wrap[data-id="'+id+'"] .wbs-exercises').sortable('destroy');
		$('.wbs-cat-wrap[data-id="'+id+'"]').remove();
		$('.cat-list>li[data-id="'+id+'"]').removeClass('selected-cat');
		wbWrap.removeClass('full-cats');
	}

	function openExInfoModal(catId,exId){
		clearExModalDescr();
		exModalContent.html(
			createExDescr(nfWB.categories[catId].exercises[exId])
		)
		$.fancybox.open({
			src  : '#ex-info-modal',
			type : 'inline'
		},{
			video: {
				autoStart: false
			}
		});
	}

	function createExDescr(e){
		title = '<h1>'+e.name+'</h1>';
		exDescrHTML = '<div class="wbec-block"><div class="wbec-text-top">'+title+'</div>';
		exDescrHTML += createWBSlider(e);
		exDescrHTML +='<div class="wbec-text-bottom">';
		exDescrHTML += title;
		exDescrHTML += e.description;
		exDescrHTML +='</div></div>';
		return exDescrHTML;
	}

	function createWBSlider(exercise) {
		var imagesEmpty = exercise.images.length ? '' : ' empty-img';
		var videosEmpty = exercise.video.length ? '' : ' empty-video';
		var WBSlider = '<div class="wbec-slider'+imagesEmpty+videosEmpty+'">\
			<div class="nf-exercise-slider-wrap">\
				<div class="nf-carousel-frame">\
					<div class="nfcf-loader"></div>';
	  WBSlider += createImgSlider(exercise.images);
		WBSlider +=	createVideoSlider(exercise.video);
		WBSlider += '</div>\
				<div class="nfcf-nav">\
					<div class="nfcf-media-types">\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-img-type active">\
							<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="17" height="13" viewBox="0 0 17 13"><defs><clipPath id="6ocea"><path fill="#fff" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z"/></clipPath><clipPath id="6oceb"><path fill="#fff" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6ocea&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6oceb&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" d="M1.727 8v0s2.093 1 4 1c1.906 0 4.062-2 6-2 1.937 0 4 1 4 1v0"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>PICS</span>\
						</a>\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-video-type">\
							<svg style="top:4px;max-width: 20px;max-height: 20px;" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="-1 -3 20 20"><defs><clipPath id="dhv7a"><path fill="#fff" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" class="v-ic-0" stroke="#a3b7d6" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#dhv7a&quot;)"/></g><g><path class="v-ic" fill="#a3b7d6" d="M10.721 7.5l-3.99 2.508V4.992z"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>VIDEO</span>\
						</a>\
					</div>\
					<div class="nfcf-carousel-nav custom-arrows">\
						<div class="media-img-nav"></div>\
						<div class="media-video-nav"></div>\
					</div>\
				</div>\
			</div>\
		</div>'
		return WBSlider
	}

	function createImgSlider(e){
		imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-images-wrap"><div class="nfcf-images">';
		imgSlides = '';
		e.forEach(function(elem){
			imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-img-slide">\
				<div class="img" style="background-image:url('+elem+')"></div>\
			</div>'
		})
		imagesSlider += imgSlides;
		imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
		return imagesSlider;
	}
	function createVideoSlider(e) {
		imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-video-wrap"><div class="nfcf-videos">';
		imgSlides = '';
		e.forEach(function(elem){
			imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-vid-slide">\
				<video controls="controls">\
          <source src="'+elem+'" type="video/mp4">\
          Your browser does not support the video tag.\
        </video>\
			</div>'
		})
		imagesSlider += imgSlides;
		imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
		return imagesSlider;
	}

	//validate time selects
	function updMaxTimeSelect() {
		var thisVal = minTimeS.val();
		maxTimeS.children().removeAttr('disabled');
	  maxTimeS.find('option[value="'+thisVal+'"]')
	    .prevAll().attr('disabled','disabled')
	  	.prevAll().attr('disabled','disabled');
	  var p = maxTimeS.parent();
	  p.find('.select-items>div').removeAttr('disabled');
	  p.find('.select-items>div[data-val="'+thisVal+'"]')
	  	.attr('disabled','disabled')
	  	.prevAll().attr('disabled','disabled');
	}
	function updMinTimeSelect() {
		var thisVal = maxTimeS.val(),
		    p = minTimeS.parent();
	  if (thisVal !== 'none') {
	  	minTimeS.children().removeAttr('disabled');
	  	minTimeS.find('option[value="'+thisVal+'"]')
	  		.attr('disabled','disabled')
	  		.nextAll().attr('disabled','disabled');
	    p.find('.select-items>div').removeAttr('disabled');
	    p.find('.select-items>div[data-val="'+thisVal+'"]')
	    	.attr('disabled','disabled')
	    	.nextAll().attr('disabled','disabled');
	  } else {
	  	minTimeS.children().removeAttr('disabled');
	  	p.find('.select-items>div').removeAttr('disabled');
	  }
	}
	body.on('change', '#min-time', updMaxTimeSelect);
	body.on('change', '#max-time', updMinTimeSelect);
  
	// validate reps and distance
	$('#reps, #distance').change(function(){
		if (+$(this).val() < 1) {
			$(this).val(1);
		}
	})
	//disable scroll on mobile for draggeble elems
	// if (window.NodeList && !NodeList.prototype.forEach) {
 //    NodeList.prototype.forEach = Array.prototype.forEach;
 //  }
	// var moveList = document.querySelectorAll('div.gg');
	// if (moveList) {
	//   moveList.forEach(function(move){
	//   	move.addEventListener('touchmove', function(e){
	//   		e.preventDefault();
	//   	});
	//   });
	// }
	}
  	$('.wb-start-content').show();

}()
/*****[/Fitness/js/nf-vt.js]******/

!function(){

	if ($('.nf-vt').length > 0) {
	// mobile html rebuilding (strange  reordering html on the mobile design)
	$('.mobile-dashbord-holder').append(
		$('.dashboard-wrapper').clone(), 
		$('.sidebar-wrap').clone()
	);

	window.nfVT = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vtState'));

	//variables
	var hideStartBlock = nfVT !== null,
			type = $('#type'),
	    mobileFieldsList = $('.fields-info-list'),
			dynamicFields = $('#dynamic-fields'),
			exModalContent = $('.exercise-modal-content'),
			exDesktopContent = $('.vt-exercise-content'),
			nfVTWrap = $('.nf-vt'),
			vtForm = $('.vt-form'),
			vtComplex = $('.vt-complex'),
			idCounter = 1,
			mobileSlider = false,
			desktopSlider = false,
			enableModal = false,
			enableOpen = false,
			val,fieldsHTML,typeText,tempHTML,p,v,t,that,tempId,fieldsAmount,exId,curEx;

	//init nfVT data (if not exist)
	if (!hideStartBlock) {
  	nfVT = {
			formStartData: {},
			lastVTComplex: false,
			useFormData: false,
			type: false,
			mobileListHelper: '',
			formDataSerialized: '',
		}
  } else {
  	if (nfVT.lastVTComplex) {
  		nfVTWrap.addClass('show-dynamic-fields show-vt-app');
  		changeVTForm(nfVT.useFormData);
  		mobileFieldsList.html(nfVT.mobileListHelper);
  		type.val(nfVT.type.value);
  		$('.type-holder .select-selected').text(nfVT.type.text);
      initVT(nfVT.lastVTComplex);
  	}
  }

	type.on('change', function(){
		val = $(this).val();
		typeText = this.options[this.selectedIndex].text;
		clearForm();
		if (val === '') {
			nfVTWrap.removeClass('show-dynamic-fields show-vt-app');
			nfVT.lastVTComplex = false;
			updateLocalStoreVT();
		} else {
			nfVTWrap.removeClass('show-vt-app');
			nfVTWrap.addClass('show-dynamic-fields');
			if (nfVT.formStartData.hasOwnProperty(val)) {
				changeVTForm(nfVT.formStartData[val]);
				nfVT.lastVTComplex = false;
				updateLocalStoreVT();
			} else {
				jQuery.ajax({
					url: val,
					type: 'get',
					dataType: 'json',
					success: function (res) {
						// console.log(res)
						nfVT.formStartData[val] = res;
						nfVT.lastVTComplex = false;
						changeVTForm(res);
						updateLocalStoreVT();
		      },
		      error: function (res) {
		        console.log('error');
		        console.log(res);
		      },
		      complete: function() {
		      	
		      }
				});
			}
			
		}
	});

	function changeVTForm(data){
		tempHTML = createFormFields(data);
		dynamicFields.html(tempHTML[0]);
		mobileFieldsList.html(tempHTML[1]);
		nfCustomSelect('custom-select d-field');
	}

	function createUserFormData(){
		var data = [];
		$('.dynamic-fields>div').each(function(i,e){
			if ($(e).hasClass('radios-type-wrap')) {
				var r = [];
				$(e).find('.custom-btn').each(function(index,el){
					var input = $(el).find('input');
					r.push(
					  {
					  	value: input.val(),
					  	text: $(el).find('.c-lable').text(),
					  	selected: input.prop('checked'),
					  }
					)
				})
				data.push(
				  {
				  	fieldType: "radios",
				  	label: $(e).find('.label').text(),
				  	name: $(e).find('input').attr('name'),
				  	radios: r,
				  	classes: $(e).attr('class'),
				  }
				)
			} else if ($(e).hasClass('select-type-wrap')) {
				data.push(createSelectObj(e))
			} else {
				var fields = [];
				$(e).find('.check-filling').each(function(ind,ele){
					fields.push(createSelectObj(ele))
				});
				data.push(
					{
						fieldType: "double-col-on-mobile",
						fields: fields,
						classes: $(e).attr('class'),
					}
				)
			}
		})
		return data;
	}

	function saveMobileList() {
		return mobileFieldsList.html()
	}

	function createSelectObj(selector) {
		var s = [];
		saveSelectOpts(s, '#'+$(selector).attr('data-id'));
		return {
			fieldType: "select",
			label: $(selector).find('label').text(),
			name: $(selector).find('select').attr('name'),
			select: s,
			classes: $(selector).attr('class')
		}
	}

	function saveSelectOpts(arr, select) {
		Array.from(document.querySelector(select).options).forEach(function(opt) {
		  arr.push(
		   {
		   	value: opt.value,
		   	text: opt.text,
		   	selected: opt.selected
		   }
		  )
		});
	}

	function createFormFields(arr) {
		fieldsHTML = '';
		mobFieldsHTMl = '<li><div class="title">Type:</div><div class="text">'+typeText+'</div></li>';
		fieldsAmount = 0;
		arr.forEach(function(e){
			switch (e.fieldType) {

			  case 'radios':
			    tempHTML = createRadios(e)
			    fieldsHTML += tempHTML[0];
			    mobFieldsHTMl += tempHTML[1];
			    break;

			  case 'select':
			  	tempHTML = createSelect(e);
			  	fieldsHTML += tempHTML[0];
			    mobFieldsHTMl += tempHTML[1];
			    break;

			  case 'double-col-on-mobile':
			  	fieldsHTML += '<div class="double-col-on-mobile">';
			  	e.fields.forEach(function(item){
			  		tempHTML = createSelect(item);
				  	fieldsHTML += tempHTML[0];
				    mobFieldsHTMl += tempHTML[1];
			  	})
			  	fieldsHTML += '</div>';
			    break;

			  default:
			    break;
			}
		})
		return [fieldsHTML,mobFieldsHTMl];
	}

	function createRadios(data) {
		var radioMobItem = '<li data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'"><div class="title">'+data.label+'</div><div class="text">--</div></li>';
		var customClass = data.hasOwnProperty('customClass') ? data.customClass + ' ' : '';
		var classes = data.hasOwnProperty('classes') ? data.classes : 'radios-type-wrap '+customClass+'check-filling';
		var radios = '<div data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'" class="'+classes+'">\
		<div class="label">'+data.label+'</div>\
		<div class="radios-type">';
		data.radios.forEach(function(e){
			var checked = e.selected ? ' checked' : '';
			radios += '<label class="custom-btn radio">\
				<input class="radio-i" name="'+data.name+'" type="radio" value="'+e.value+'"'+checked+'>\
				<span class="c-lable">'+e.text+'</span>\
			</label>';
			if (checked) {
				radioMobItem = '<li data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'"><div class="title">'+data.label+'</div><div class="text">'+e.text+'</div></li>';
			}
		});
		radios += '</div></div>';
		idCounter++;
		fieldsAmount++;
		return [radios,radioMobItem];
	}

	function createSelect(data) {
		var radioMobItem = '<li data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'"><div class="title">'+data.label+'</div><div class="text">--</div></li>';
		var customClass = data.hasOwnProperty('customClass') ? data.customClass + ' ' : '';
		var classes = data.hasOwnProperty('classes') ? data.classes : 'select-type-wrap '+customClass+'check-filling';
		var select = '<div data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'" class="'+classes+'">\
		<label for="equipment">'+data.label+'</label>\
		<div class="custom-select d-field trigger-event">\
			<select id="vt-id'+idCounter+'" name="'+data.name+'">';
		
		data.select.forEach(function(e){
			var selected = e.selected ? ' selected' : '';
			select += '<option value="'+e.value+'"'+selected+'>'+e.text+'</option>';
			if (selected) {
				radioMobItem = '<li data-id="vt-id'+idCounter+'"><div class="title">'+data.label+'</div><div class="text">'+e.text+'</div></li>';
			}
		});
		select += '</select></div></div>';
		idCounter++;
		fieldsAmount++;
		return [select,radioMobItem];
	}

	function clearForm() {
		mobileFieldsList.html('<li><div class="title">Type:</div><div class="text">--</div></li>');
		dynamicFields.html('');
	}

	dynamicFields.on('change', 'input[type="radio"], select', function(){
		that = $(this);
		v = that.val(); //value
	  p = that.parents('.check-filling'); //main parent
	  t = that.hasClass('radio-i') ? that.parent().find('.c-lable').text() : this.options[this.selectedIndex].text; //text
	  $('.fields-info-list>li[data-id="'+p.attr('data-id')+'"]>.text').text(t);
		p.toggleClass('vt-filled', v !== '');
		if (fieldsAmount === $('.vt-filled').length) {
			showVTComplex()
		} else {
			nfVTWrap.removeClass('show-vt-app');
			nfVT.lastVTComplex = false;
			updateLocalStoreVT();
		}
	});

	vtForm.submit(function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
	});

	function showVTComplex() {
		nfVTWrap.addClass('show-vt-app');
		// ajax not working in static markup, needed at least webserver which you can run via grunt
		// 'post' not working without server, so we made 'get'
		// replace type 'get' to 'post' and uncomment 'data'
		nfVT.formDataSerialized = vtForm.serialize();
		jQuery.ajax({
			url: vtForm.attr('action'),
			type: 'post',
			data: nfVT.formDataSerialized,
			dataType: 'json',
			success: function (res) {
        // console.log('success');
        // console.log(res);
        nfVT.lastVTComplex = res;
        nfVT.useFormData = createUserFormData();
        nfVT.mobileListHelper = saveMobileList();
        nfVT.type = {
        	value: type.val(),
        	text: $( "#type option:selected" ).text()
        };
        updateLocalStoreVT();
        initVT(res);
     //    desktopSlider = '.vt-exercise-content';
			  // initSlider(desktopSlider);

      },
      error: function (res) {
        console.log('error');
        console.log(res);
      },
      complete: function() {
        
      }
		});
	}

	

	////show exercise descr event
	vtComplex.on('click', '.exercise', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		// if (!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
			$('.exercise.active').removeClass('active');
			$(this).addClass('active');
			var idArr = $(this).attr('href').split('_');
			var descr = createExDescr(nfVT.lastVTComplex[idArr[0]].exercises[idArr[1]]);
			clearExDesktopDescr();
			updateDesktopDescr(descr);
		// }
		if (!enableModal) {
			
			if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
				clearExModalDescr();
				updateMobileDescr(descr);
				if (!enableOpen) {
					curEx = +$('.exercise.active').attr('data-id');
					$.fancybox.open({
						src  : '#ex-info-modal',
						type : 'inline',
						opts: {
							afterClose : function() {
								enableOpen = false;
								enableModal = false;
							}
						}
					},{
						video: {
							autoStart: false
						}
					});
				}
				enableOpen = false;
				// openExInfoModal(tempIds[0],tempIds[1]);
				// mobileSlider = '#ex-info-modal';
				// initSlider(mobileSlider);
			}
		}
		enableModal = false;
	});

	$('.prev-ex,.next-ex').click(function(){
		enableOpen = true;
		if ($(this).hasClass('prev-ex')) {
			if (curEx <= 1) {
				curEx = exId -1;
				$('.exercise[data-id="'+curEx+'"]').trigger('click');
			} else {
				curEx--;
				$('.exercise[data-id="'+curEx+'"]').trigger('click');
			}
		} else {
			if (curEx + 1 >= exId) {
				curEx = 1;
				$('.exercise[data-id="1"]').trigger('click');
			} else {
				curEx++;
				$('.exercise[data-id="'+curEx+'"]').trigger('click');
			}
		}
	})

	function initVT(data){
		clearExDesktopDescr();
		clearExModalDescr();
		vtComplex.html(createCats(data));
		enableModal = true;
		setTimeout(function(){
			$('.exercise').first().trigger('click');
		})
	};

	function updateDesktopDescr(descr) {
		exDesktopContent.html(descr);
		desktopSlider = '.vt-exercise-content';
	  initSlider(desktopSlider);
	}

	function updateMobileDescr(descr) {
		exModalContent.html(descr);
		mobileSlider = '#ex-info-modal';
		initSlider(mobileSlider);
	}

	function createCats(data) {
		var cats = '';
		exId = 1;
		data.forEach(function(e,i){
			cats += '<div class="cat-wrap" data-id="'+i+'">\
			<div class="cat-head">\
				<div class="cat">'+e.category+'</div>\
				<div class="reps">REPS</div>\
			</div>\
			<div class="exercises">';
			cats += createExs(e.exercises,i);						
			cats += '</div></div>';
		});
		return cats;
	}

	function createExs(arr,catIndex) {
		var exs = '';
		var reps;
		arr.forEach(function(e,i){
			reps = e.hasOwnProperty('repsVal') ? e.repsVal : '';
			exs += '<a data-id="'+exId+'" class="exercise" href="'+catIndex+'_'+i+'">\
				<div>\
					<div class="title">'+e.name+'</div>\
				  <div class="rep">'+reps+'</div>\
				</div>\
			</a>';
			exId++;
		})
		return exs;
	}

	//exercise descr
	function createExDescr(e){
		var title = '<h1>'+e.name+'</h1>';
		var exDescrHTML = '<div class="wbec-block"><div class="wbec-text-top">'+title+'</div>';
		exDescrHTML += createWBSlider(e);
		exDescrHTML +='<div class="wbec-text-bottom">';
		exDescrHTML += title;
		exDescrHTML += e.description;
		exDescrHTML +='</div></div>';
		return exDescrHTML;
	}

	function createWBSlider(exercise) {
		var imagesEmpty = exercise.images.length ? '' : ' empty-img';
		var videosEmpty = exercise.video.length ? '' : ' empty-video';
		var WBSlider = '<div class="wbec-slider'+imagesEmpty+videosEmpty+'">\
			<div class="nf-exercise-slider-wrap">\
				<div class="nf-carousel-frame">\
					<div class="nfcf-loader"></div>';
	  WBSlider += createImgSlider(exercise.images);
		WBSlider +=	createVideoSlider(exercise.video);
		WBSlider += '</div>\
				<div class="nfcf-nav">\
					<div class="nfcf-media-types">\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-img-type active">\
							<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="17" height="13" viewBox="0 0 17 13"><defs><clipPath id="6ocea"><path fill="#fff" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z"/></clipPath><clipPath id="6oceb"><path fill="#fff" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6ocea&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6oceb&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" d="M1.727 8v0s2.093 1 4 1c1.906 0 4.062-2 6-2 1.937 0 4 1 4 1v0"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>PICS</span>\
						</a>\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-video-type">\
							<svg style="top:4px;max-width: 20px;max-height: 20px;" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="-1 -3 20 20"><defs><clipPath id="dhv7a"><path fill="#fff" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" class="v-ic-0" stroke="#a3b7d6" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#dhv7a&quot;)"/></g><g><path class="v-ic" fill="#a3b7d6" d="M10.721 7.5l-3.99 2.508V4.992z"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>VIDEO</span>\
						</a>\
					</div>\
					<div class="nfcf-carousel-nav custom-arrows">\
						<div class="media-img-nav"></div>\
						<div class="media-video-nav"></div>\
					</div>\
				</div>\
			</div>\
		</div>'
		return WBSlider
	}

	function createImgSlider(e){
		var imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-images-wrap"><div class="nfcf-images">';
		var imgSlides = '';
		e.forEach(function(elem){
			imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-img-slide">\
				<div class="img" style="background-image:url('+elem+')"></div>\
			</div>'
		})
		imagesSlider += imgSlides;
		imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
		return imagesSlider;
	}
	function createVideoSlider(e) {
		var imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-video-wrap"><div class="nfcf-videos">';
		var imgSlides = '';
		e.forEach(function(elem){
			imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-vid-slide">\
				<video controls="controls">\
          <source src="'+elem+'" type="video/mp4">\
          Your browser does not support the video tag.\
        </video>\
			</div>'
		})
		imagesSlider += imgSlides;
		imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
		return imagesSlider;
	}

	//init exercises slider
  function initSlider(parent) {
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-images').slick({
	  	fade: true,
	  	dots: true,
	  	infinite: false,
	  	prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			appendArrows: parent+' .media-img-nav',
			appendDots: parent+' .media-img-nav'
		});
		$(parent+' .nfcf-videos').slick({
	  	fade: true,
	  	dots: true,
	  	infinite: false,
	  	prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
			appendArrows: parent+' .media-video-nav',
			appendDots: parent+' .media-video-nav'
		}).on('beforeChange', function(e, slick, currentIndex) {
			var currentSlide = jQuery(slick.$slides[currentIndex]);
			var currVideo = currentSlide.find('video');
			if (currVideo.length) {
				currVideo[0].pause();
			}
		})
  }

	//destroy exercises slider
  function destroySlider(parent) {
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-images').slick('unslick');
  	$(parent+' .nfcf-videos').slick('unslick');
  }

  function clearExModalDescr(){
  	if (mobileSlider) {
  		destroySlider(mobileSlider)
  	}
  	exModalContent.html('');
  }

  function clearExDesktopDescr(){
  	if (desktopSlider) {
  		destroySlider(desktopSlider);
  		desktopSlider = false;
  	}
  	exDesktopContent.html('');
  }

	function clearVTComplex(){
		vtComplex.html('')
	}

	//change video type
	$('body').on('click', '.btn-media-type', function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		if (!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
			var p = $(this).parents('div.nf-exercise-slider-wrap');
			p.toggleClass('nf-show-video');
			p.find('a.btn-media-type').toggleClass('active');
			if ($(this).hasClass('btn-img-type')) {
				p.find('video').each(function(){
					this.pause()
				})
			}
		}
	});
	$('body').on('keyup', '.btn-media-type, .slick-arrow', function(e){
		var code = (e.keyCode ? e.keyCode : e.which);
		if(code == 13) { //Enter keycode
		  $(this).click();
		}
	});

	//open form fields on mobile =============================
	$('.open-form-fields').click(function(e){
		e.preventDefault();
		$.fancybox.open({
			src  : '#vt-fields',
			type : 'inline',
			opts : {
				afterClose : function() {
					
					// form.trigger('submit');
				}
			}
		},{
    	baseClass : 'vt-fancy-form',
    	// touch: false,
		});
	});

	//clear vmb data
	$('.clear-vt-data').click(function(){
		localStorage.removeItem('vtState');
		location.reload();
	});

	//save nfWB to localStorage
	var updateLocalStoreVT = function(){
		try {
		  localStorage.setItem('vtState', JSON.stringify(nfVT));
		} catch (e) {
		  if (e == QUOTA_EXCEEDED_ERR) {
		   console.log('Limit is exceeded');
		  }
		}
	}
	}

}()
/*****[/Fitness/js/nf-vmb.js]******/

!function () {

	if ($('.nf-vmb').length > 0) {
		// mobile html rebuilding (strange  reordering html on the mobile design)
		$('.mobile-dashbord-holder').append(
			$('.dashboard-wrapper').clone(),
			$('.sidebar-wrap').clone()
		);

		//app ===================================
		//app ===================================
		//app ===================================
		var vmbStartBlock = $('.vmb-start-content'),
			vmbApp = $('.vmb-app');

		window.nfVMB = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vmbState'));
		var hideStartBlock = nfVMB !== null,

			// nodes
			printBody = $('.print-body'),

			//helpers
			printContent = '',
			id;

		if (!hideStartBlock) {
			nfVMB = {
				mealPlan: false,
				currentCalories: 0,
				formData: [], //[ [fieldName, value, text], [fieldName, value, text], ... ]
				formDataSerialized: '',
			}
		}

		var mealsWrap = $('.meals-wrap'),
			caloriesMinWrap = $('.cal-min'),
			caloriesMaxWrap = $('.cal-max'),
			mobileFieldsList = $('.fields-info-list .text'),
			formFields = $('.js-handler'),
			tempMealCurrent,
			tempMealAmount,
			catsList,
			mealCatsCounter,
			createMealTempFoodList,
			mealAmountTempClass;

		//clear vmb data
		$('.clear-vmb-data').click(function () {
			localStorage.removeItem('vmbState');
			location.reload();
		})

		var createCatFields = function (chosenFood) {
			var catFieldsHTML = '';
			chosenFood.forEach(function (e) {
				catFieldsHTML += '<div data-id="' + e.id + '" class="cat-field">\
													<span class="cat-amount">(' + e.amount + ') </span>'
					+ e.name + '</div>';
			});
			return catFieldsHTML;
		}

		var createMealCats = function (cats, index) {
			catsList = '<ul class="meal-cats">';
			cats.forEach(function (e, i) {
				tempMealCurrent = e.hasOwnProperty('currentCount') ? e.currentCount : 0;
				createMealTempFoodList = e.hasOwnProperty('food') ? createCatFields(e.food) : '';
				
				disabled = '';
				//console.log(e);
				//tempMealAmount = e.maxCount ? '<div class="cur">' + tempMealCurrent + '</div><div class="max">' + e.maxCount + '</div>' : '<div class="infinity"></div>';
				tempMealAmount = e.maxCount ? '<div class="cur">' + tempMealCurrent + '</div>/<div class="max">' + e.maxCount + '</div>' : '<div class="zero">-</div>';
				if(e.count == "~"){
					tempMealAmount = '<div class="infinity"></div>';
				}
				
				mealAmountTempClass = '';
				if (tempMealCurrent) {
					mealAmountTempClass = 'active'
					if (e.maxCount === 0) {
						mealAmountTempClass = 'full'
						disabled = ' data-disabled="true" ';
					}
					if (e.maxCount === tempMealCurrent) {
						mealAmountTempClass = 'full'
					}
					if (e.count == "~") {
						mealAmountTempClass = 'active'
						disabled = '';
					}
				}
				catsList += '<li>\
										<a' + disabled + ' data-meal-index="' + index + '" data-index="' + i + '" id="mcl-' + mealCatsCounter + '" href="' +
					e.foodListUrl + '" class="meal-cat-link">\
											<div class="meal-cat"><span class="mc-name">' + e.label +
					'</span><div class="meal-amount ' + mealAmountTempClass + '">'
					+ tempMealAmount +
					'</div></div>\
											<div class="meal-cat-fields">' + createMealTempFoodList + '</div>\
										</a>\
									</li>';
				mealCatsCounter++;
			})
			catsList += '</ul>'
			return catsList
		}

		var pickRecipe = function (meal, mealArray, index, loadCache) {

			var calorieMin = 0;//Math.floor(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMin / mealArray.length) - 100;
			var calorieMax = Math.floor(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMax / mealArray.length) + 100;
			var mealLabel = meal.label;

			if (mealLabel.includes("Snack")) {
				mealLabel = "Snack";
			}
			var currMeal = nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[index];
			if (loadCache && currMeal.recipes && currMeal.recipes.length) {

				recipeList = '<ul class="meal-cats">';
			
				if(currMeal.recipes)
				{
				currMeal.recipes.forEach(function (e, i) {
					//currentCalories += e.calories;
					recipeList += '<li>\
											<a data-meal-index="' + index + '" data-index="' + i + '" id="mcl-' + mealCatsCounter + '" class="meal-cat-link" href="' + e.link + '" target="_blank">\
												<div class="meal-image"><img src="' + e.image + '" /></div>\
												<div class="meal-cat"><span class="mc-name">' + e.name + '</span><div class="meal-amount"></div></div>\
											</a>\
										</li>';
					mealCatsCounter++;
				});
				}
				recipeList += '</ul>';

				jQuery('.recipe-' + index).html(recipeList);

			//	nfVMB.currentCalories = currentCalories;

				index++;
				if (mealArray[index]) {
					pickRecipe(mealArray[index], mealArray, index, loadCache);
				}

			} else {

				jQuery.ajax({
					url: '/data/recipe',
					//type: 'post',
					type: 'get',
					data: {
						meal: mealLabel,
						currentCalories: nfVMB.currentCalories,
						calorieMin: calorieMin,
						calorieMax: calorieMax
					},
					dataType: 'json',
					success: function (res) {
						//console.log('success');
						//console.log(res);

						nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[index].recipes = res;

						recipeList = '<ul class="meal-cats">';

						nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[index].recipes.forEach(function (e, i) {
							//currentCalories += e.calories;
							recipeList += '<li>\
											<a data-meal-index="' + index + '" data-index="' + i + '" id="mcl-' + mealCatsCounter + '" class="meal-cat-link" href="' + e.link + '" target="_blank">\
												<div class="meal-image"><img src="' + e.image + '" /></div>\
												<div class="meal-cat"><span class="mc-name">' + e.name + '</span><div class="meal-amount"></div></div>\
											</a>\
										</li>';
							mealCatsCounter++;
						});
						recipeList += '</ul>';
						jQuery('.recipe-' + index).html(recipeList);

						//nfVMB.currentCalories = currentCalories;

						updateLocalStoreVMB();


						index++;
						if (mealArray[index]) {
							pickRecipe(mealArray[index], mealArray, index);
						}

					},
					error: function (res, a, b) {
						console.log('error');
						console.log(res);
					},
					complete: function () {
						//vmbFormSending = false;
						//submitBtn.removeClass('loading')
					}
				});
			}

		}

		var createMeals = function (loadCache = false) {

			var meals = '';
			var mealArray = [];
			mealCatsCounter = 0;
			currentCalories = 0;

			nfVMB.currentCalories = currentCalories;
			updateLocalStoreVMB();

			nfVMB.mealPlan.meals.forEach(function (e, index) {
				meals += '<div class="meal-block">\
							<div class="meal-name-wrap">\
								<div class="ic">' +
					(e.hasOwnProperty('icon') ? '<img src="' + e.icon + '" alt="img">' : '')
					+ '</div>\
								<div class="meal-name">'+ e.label + '</div>\
							</div>';
				
				meals += createMealCats(e.categories, index)
				//meals += '<div class="recipe-' + index + '"></div>';

				mealArray.push(e);
				//pickRecipe(e, index);

				meals += '</div>';
			});

			if (mealArray.length > 0) {
				setTimeout(function () {
					pickRecipe(mealArray[0], mealArray, 0, loadCache);
				}, 200);
			}


			return meals;
		}


		//form =================================
		var form = $('.vmb-form'),
			url = form.attr('action'),
			submitBtn = $('.submit-vmb'),
			vmbFormSending = false,
			tempFormData,
			elem;

		var updFormData = function () {

			tempFormData = [];

			formFields.each(function (i, e) {
				elem = $(e);
				tempFormData.push([
					elem.attr('name'),
					elem.val(),
					elem.find('option:selected').text()
				])
			});

			nfVMB.formData = tempFormData;
			nfVMB.formDataSerialized = form.serialize();

		}

		var updMobileFieldsList = function () {
			mobileFieldsList.each(function (i, e) {
				if(nfVMB.formData[i]){
					$(e).text(nfVMB.formData[i][2]);
				}
			});
		}

		var updFormValues = function () {
			formFields.each(function (i, e) {
				$(this).val(nfVMB.formData[i][1])
					.parent().find('.select-selected').text(nfVMB.formData[i][2])
			});
			updMobileFieldsList()
		}

		var updateLocalStoreVMB = function () {
			try {
				localStorage.setItem('vmbState', JSON.stringify(nfVMB));
			} catch (e) {
				if (e == QUOTA_EXCEEDED_ERR) {
					console.log('Limit is exceeded');
				}
			}
		}

		//show vmb if has stored data
		if (hideStartBlock) {
			caloriesMinWrap.text(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMin)
			caloriesMaxWrap.text(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMax)
			mealsWrap.html(createMeals(true));
			vmbStartBlock.hide();
			vmbApp.show();
			updFormValues();
		}

		form.submit(function (e) {

			e.preventDefault();
			if (vmbFormSending) {
				return false;
			}

			updFormData();
			vmbFormSending = true;
			submitBtn.addClass('loading');
			// ajax not working in static markup, needed at least webserver which you can run via grunt
			// 'post' not working without server, so we made 'get'
			// replace type 'get' to 'post' and uncomment 'data'
			jQuery.ajax({
				url: url,
				type: 'post',
				//type: 'get',
				data: nfVMB.formDataSerialized,
				dataType: 'json',
				success: function (res) {
					// console.log('success');
					// console.log(res);
					nfVMB.mealPlan = res;
					nfVMB.currentCalories = 0;

					caloriesMinWrap.text(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMin)
					caloriesMaxWrap.text(nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMax)
					mealsWrap.html(createMeals());
					vmbStartBlock.hide();
					vmbApp.show();

					updateLocalStoreVMB()

				},
				error: function (res) {
					console.log('error');
					console.log(res);
				},
				complete: function () {
					vmbFormSending = false;
					submitBtn.removeClass('loading')
				}
			});
			return false;
		});

		// food list modal ====================================
		var foodModalLoading = false,
			scrollBar,
			foodListHTML,
			foodCountHolder,
			foodAmountInput,
			foodAmountValue,
			foodTempCurrent,
			foodTempMax,
			mealIndex,
			catLinkIndex,
			tempLinkCount,
			tempMealCatFields,
			caloriesDelta,
			foodModal = $('#food-modal'),
			foodTypeWrap = $('.food-type'),
			foodTypeCur = $('.f-type-cur'),
			foodTypeMax = $('.f-type-max'),
			foodListWrap = $('.food-list-main-wrap'),
			foodTypeCountWrap = $('.food-type-count');

		// add/subtract food logic
		foodModal.on('click', '.food-count>a', function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			foodCountHolder = $(this).parent();
			foodAmountInput = foodCountHolder.find('.food-amount');
			foodAmountValue = +foodAmountInput.text();
			caloriesDelta = +foodAmountInput.attr('data-cal');

			if ($(this).hasClass('plus')) {
				//increase
				foodAmountValue++;
				foodTempCurrent++;

				if (foodTempCurrent > foodTempMax) {
					foodAmountValue--;
					foodTempCurrent--;
					return;
				}
				nfVMB.currentCalories += caloriesDelta;
				//check calories
				if (nfVMB.currentCalories > nfVMB.mealPlan.calorieMax) {
					nfVMB.currentCalories -= caloriesDelta;
					foodAmountValue--;
					foodTempCurrent--;
					alert('Exceeded maximum calories limit. Current calories: ' + nfVMB.currentCalories)
					return;
				}
				if (foodTempCurrent === foodTempMax) {
					foodTypeCountWrap.addClass('full')
				}
				foodCountHolder.addClass('not-empty');
			} else {
				//decrease	
				if (foodAmountValue) {
					foodAmountValue--;
					foodTempCurrent--;
					if (!foodAmountValue) {
						foodCountHolder.removeClass('not-empty');
					}
					foodTypeCountWrap.removeClass('full');
					nfVMB.currentCalories -= caloriesDelta;
				}
			}
			// console.log(foodTempCurrent);
			foodAmountInput.text(foodAmountValue);
			foodTypeCur.text(foodTempCurrent);
		});

		var createFoodList = function (foodList) {
			foodListHTML = '<div class="food-list-wrap"><ul class="food-list">';
			foodList.forEach(function (e) {
				foodListHTML +=
					'<li data-id="' + e.id + '">\
				<div class="food-name">' + e.name + '</div>\
				<div class="food-count">\
					<a href="#" class="minus"></a>\
					<div class="food-amount" data-cal="' + e.calories + '">0</div>\
					<a href="#" class="plus"></a>\
				</div>\
			</li>';
			})
			foodListHTML += '</ul></div>';
			return foodListHTML;
		}

		var createModal = function (id, foodList) {
			var l = $('#' + id), //clicked meal-cat-link
				catId = +l.attr('data-index'),
				mealId = +l.attr('data-meal-index');
			foodTempCurrent = +l.find('.cur').text();
			foodTempMax = +l.find('.max').text();

			foodTypeCountWrap.toggleClass('infinit', !foodTempMax)

			if (!foodTempMax) {
				foodTempMax = Infinity;
				foodTempCurrent = 10000000;
			}

			foodTypeWrap.text(l.find('.mc-name').text());
			foodTypeCur.text(foodTempCurrent);
			foodTypeMax.text(foodTempMax);
			foodListWrap.html(createFoodList(foodList));
			foodTypeCountWrap.removeClass('full');

			if (nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealId].categories[catId].hasOwnProperty('food')) {
				nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealId].categories[catId].food.forEach(function (e) {
					$('.food-list>li[data-id="' + e.id + '"] .food-amount')
						.text(e.amount)
						.parent().addClass('not-empty')
				});

				foodTypeCountWrap.toggleClass(
					'full',
					nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealId].categories[catId].currentCount ===
					nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealId].categories[catId].maxCount
				)
			}

		}

		var chosenFoodArr;
		var getChosenFood = function () {
			var arr = [];
			$('.food-count.not-empty').each(function (i, e) {
				var p = $(e).parent();
				arr.push({
					id: +p.attr('data-id'),
					name: p.find('.food-name').text(),
					amount: +p.find('.food-amount').text()
				})
			});
			return arr;
		}

		vmbApp.on('click', '.meal-cat-link', function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			var link = $(this);
			if (foodModalLoading) {
				return false;
			}
			
			if(link.attr('data-disabled')=="true") {
				return false;
			}
			
			mealIndex = link.attr('data-meal-index');
			catLinkIndex = link.attr('data-index');
			tempMealCatFields = link.find('.meal-cat-fields');
			foodModalLoading = true;
			
			jQuery.ajax({
				url: link.attr('href'),
				type: 'get',
				dataType: 'json',
				success: function (res) {
					// console.log('success');
					// console.log(res);

					createModal(link.attr('id'), res);

					$.fancybox.open({
						src: '#food-modal',
						type: 'inline',
						opts: {
							afterShow: function () {
								scrollBar = new SimpleBar($('.food-list-wrap')[0]);
								if ($('.food-list-wrap .simplebar-vertical').css('visibility') === 'visible') {
									$('.vmb-food-modal .simplebar-content-wrapper').css('padding-right', '30px')
								}
							},
							afterClose: function () {
								scrollBar.unMount();

								chosenFoodArr = getChosenFood();
								tempMealCatFields.html(createCatFields(chosenFoodArr));

								// console.log('cal: '+nfVMB.currentCalories);

								nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealIndex].categories[catLinkIndex].food = chosenFoodArr;
								nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealIndex].categories[catLinkIndex].currentCount = foodTempCurrent;
								tempLinkCount = link.find('.meal-amount');
								tempLinkCount.find('.cur').text(foodTempCurrent)
								tempLinkCount
									.toggleClass('full', foodTempCurrent === foodTempMax)
									.toggleClass('active', foodTempCurrent > 0 && foodTempCurrent < foodTempMax);
								updateLocalStoreVMB();
								pickRecipe(nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[mealIndex], nfVMB.mealPlan.meals, mealIndex, false);
							}
						}
					}, {
						baseClass: 'vmb-food-modal',
						// touch: false,
					});

				},
				error: function (res) {
					console.log('error');
					console.log(res);
				},
				complete: function () {
					foodModalLoading = false;
				}
			});
			
		})
		
		//open form fields on mobile =============================
		$('.open-form-fields').click(function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			$.fancybox.open({
				src: '#vmb-fields',
				type: 'inline',
				opts: {
					afterClose: function () {
						updFormData();
						updMobileFieldsList();
						form.trigger('submit');
					}
				}
			}, {
				baseClass: 'vmb-fancy min-close-btn',
				// touch: false,
			});
		});


		var optarray = $("#cur-weight").children('option').map(function () {
			return {
				"dataGender": $(this).attr("data-gender"),
				"dataGoal": $(this).attr("data-goal"),
				"value": this.value,
				"option": "<option value='" + this.value + "'>" + this.text + "</option>"
			}
		});

		//console.log(optarray);

		var updateWeight = function () {
			currWeight = $("#cur-weight").val();
			currGender = $("#gender").val();
			currGoal = $("#goal").val();

			$("#cur-weight").children('option').remove();

			var addoptarr = [];
			for (i = 0; i < optarray.length; i++) {
				if (optarray[i].dataGender == currGender && optarray[i].dataGoal == currGoal) {
					addoptarr.push(optarray[i].option);
				}
			}
			$("#cur-weight").html(addoptarr.join(''));

			//$("#cur-weight").val(currWeight);

			$('#cur-weight option[value=' + currWeight + ']').attr('selected', 'selected');

			nfCustomSelect('custom-select', true);
		}


		$("#gender").change(function () {
			updateWeight();
		}).change();

		$("#goal").change(function () {
			updateWeight();
		}).change();



		//make print version before print
		var enableMakePrintContent = true;
		window.onbeforeprint = function () {
			if (enableMakePrintContent) {
				makePrintContent();
			}
			enableMakePrintContent = true;
		};

		//set current date to print modal
		var curDate = new Date();
		$('.print-date').text(curDate.toLocaleDateString("en-US", { year: '2-digit', day: 'numeric', month: 'numeric' }))

		$('.show-mb-download-modal').click(function () {
			makePrintContent();
			openPrintModal();
		});

		$('.show-mb-shopping-modal').click(function () {
			makePrintContent(true);
			openPrintModal();
		});


		$('#print-modal .btn-light').click(function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			enableMakePrintContent = false;
			window.print();
		})

		function createPrintCat(index, simple) {
			//printCatAmount++;
			var printCat = '';
				if(simple){
					printCat = '<div class="print-cat-wrap">\
						<div class="">'+ createPrintExs(index, simple) + '</div>\
					</div>';
				}else{
					printCat = '<div class="print-cat-wrap">\
						<div class="print-cat-head">\
							<div class="print-cat">'+ nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[index].label + '</div>\
							<!--<div class="print-cat-notes">Notes</div>-->\
						</div>\
						<div class="">'+ createPrintExs(index, simple) + '</div>\
					</div>';
				}
			return printCat;
		}
		function createPrintExs(index, simple) {
			var exs = '';
			var meal = nfVMB.mealPlan.meals[index];
		
			if(!meal || !meal.recipes)
			{
				return exs;
			}
			meal.recipes.forEach(function (e, i) {
				//printExAmount++;
				i++;
				if(simple){
					exs += '<div class="">\
						<div class="pex-head">\
							<div class="pex-name"><h1>'+ e.name + '</h1></div>\
						</div>\
						<div class="pex-body">\
							<div class="pex-text">'+ e.ingredients + '</div>\
						</div>\
					</div>';
				}else{
					exs += '<div class="">\
						<div class="pex-head">\
							<div class="pex-name"><h1>'+ e.name + '</h1></div>\
						</div>\
						<div class="pex-body">\
							<div class="pex-images"><div><img src="' + e.image + '" alt="img"></div></div>\
							<div class="pex-text"><h2>Ingredients</h2>'+ e.ingredients + '<h2>Directions</h2>' + e.directions + '<h2>Notes</h2>' + e.notes + '</div>\
						</div>\
					</div>';
				}
			});
			return exs;
		}

		function printImages(arr) {
			var images = '';
			arr.forEach(function (elem, index) {
				images += '<div><img src="' + elem + '" alt="img"></div>'
			})
			return images;
		}

		function makePrintContent(simple = false) {
			printContent = '';

			if (nfVMB.mealPlan.meals.length) {
				nfVMB.mealPlan.meals.forEach(function (e, i) {
					id = e[0]
					newExs = e[1] === undefined ? [] : e[1];
					printContent += createPrintCat(i, simple);
				});
			} else {
				printContent = '<h2 style="text-align:center;">Empty</h2>';
			}

			printBody.html(printContent);
		}


		function openPrintModal() {
			$.fancybox.open({
				src: '#print-modal',
				type: 'inline',
			});
		}

	}

}()
/*****[/Fitness/js/nf-ml.js]******/

!function(){

	if ($('.nf-ml').length > 0) {
		// mobile html rebuilding (strange  reordering html on the mobile design)
		$('.mobile-dashbord-holder').append(
			$('.dashboard-wrapper').clone(),
			$('.sidebar-wrap').clone()
		);

		window.nfML = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('mlState20220802'));

		//variables
		var hideStartBlock = nfML !== null,
			mobileSlider = false,
			desktopSlider = false,
			enableModal = false,
			enableOpen = false,
			exModalContent = $('.exercise-modal-content'),
			exDesktopContent = $('.ml-exercise-content'),
			mlForm = $('.ml-form'),
			mlComplex = $('.ml-complex'),
			mlFields = $('#dynamic-fields'),
			mobListWrap = $('.fields-info-list'),
			id;


		//init nfML data (if not exist)
		if (!hideStartBlock) {
			nfML = {
				formData: nfFormData,
			}
			updateMLForm(nfFormData)
		} else {
			updateMLForm(nfML.formData)
		}
		showMLComplex();

		function updateMLForm(data) {
			var typeSelect = '<div class="input-holder"><label for="type">Series:</label><div class="custom-select trigger-event"><select class="js-handler" id="type" name="type">',
				catSelect = '<div class="input-holder"><label for="category">CATEGORY:</label><div class="custom-select trigger-event"><select class="js-handler" id="category" name="category">',
				selected,
				mobList = '';
			data.forEach(function (e) {
				if (e.type.selected) {
					selected = ' selected ';
					mobList += '<li><div class="title">Type:</div><div class="text">' + e.type.text + '</div></li>'
				} else {
					selected = ' ';
				}
				typeSelect += '<option' + selected + 'value="' + e.type.value + '">' + e.type.text + '</option>';
				if (e.type.selected) {
					e.category.forEach(function (elem) {
						if (elem.selected) {
							selected = ' selected ';
							mobList += '<li><div class="title">Category:</div><div class="text">' + elem.text + '</div></li>'
						} else {
							selected = ' ';
						}
						catSelect += '<option' + selected + 'value="' + elem.value + '">' + elem.text + '</option>';
					})
				}
			});
			typeSelect += '</select></div></div>';
			catSelect += '</select></div></div>';
			mlFields.html(typeSelect + catSelect);
			mobListWrap.html(mobList);
			nfCustomSelect('custom-select');
		}

		mlFields.on('change', '.custom-select>select', function () {
			var typeVal = $('#type').val();
			var catVal = $('#category').val();
			var enableSelect = true;

			//update form data arr
			if ($(this).attr('id') === 'type') {
				nfML.formData.forEach(function (e) {
					e.type.selected = false;
					e.category.forEach(function (e) {
						e.selected = false;
					})
					if (e.type.value === typeVal) {
						e.type.selected = true;
						e.category[0].selected = true;
					}
				});
			} else {
				nfML.formData.forEach(function (e) {
					e.category.forEach(function (e) {
						e.selected = false;
						if (e.value === catVal) {
							e.selected = true
						}
					})
				});
			}

			updateLocalStoreML();
			updateMLForm(nfML.formData);
			showMLComplex();
		});


		$('.prev-ex,.next-ex').click(function () {
			enableOpen = true;
			if ($(this).hasClass('prev-ex')) {
				if (id == 0) {
					$('.exercise:last-child').trigger('click');
				} else {
					$('.exercise[href="' + (id - 1) + '"]').trigger('click');
				}
			} else {
				if (id == nfML.exercisesData.exercises.length - 1) {
					$('.exercise[href="0"]').trigger('click');
				} else {
					$('.exercise[href="' + (id + 1) + '"]').trigger('click');
				}
			}
		})



		function updateDesktopDescr(descr) {
			exDesktopContent.html(descr);
			desktopSlider = '.ml-exercise-content';
			initSlider(desktopSlider);
		}

		function updateMobileDescr(descr) {
			exModalContent.html(descr);
			mobileSlider = '#ex-info-modal';
			initSlider(mobileSlider);
		}



		//exercise descr
		function createExDescr(e) {
			var title = '<h1>' + e.name + '</h1>';
			var exDescrHTML = '<div class="wbec-block"><div class="wbec-text-top">' + title + '</div>';
			exDescrHTML += createWBSlider(e);
			exDescrHTML += '<div class="wbec-text-bottom">';
			exDescrHTML += title;
			exDescrHTML += e.description;
			exDescrHTML += '</div></div>';
			return exDescrHTML;
		}

		function createWBSlider(exercise) {
			var imagesEmpty = exercise.images.length ? '' : ' empty-img';
			var videosEmpty = exercise.video.length ? '' : ' empty-video';
			var WBSlider = '<div class="wbec-slider' + imagesEmpty + videosEmpty + '">\
			<div class="nf-exercise-slider-wrap">\
				<div class="nf-carousel-frame">\
					<div class="nfcf-loader"></div>';
			WBSlider += createImgSlider(exercise.images);
			WBSlider += createVideoSlider(exercise.video);
			WBSlider += '</div>\
				<div class="nfcf-nav">\
					<div class="nfcf-media-types">\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-img-type active">\
							<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="17" height="13" viewBox="0 0 17 13"><defs><clipPath id="6ocea"><path fill="#fff" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z"/></clipPath><clipPath id="6oceb"><path fill="#fff" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M1.727 13a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V1a1 1 0 0 1 1-1h14a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11a1 1 0 0 1-1 1z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6ocea&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M3.727 4a1 1 0 1 1 2 0 1 1 0 0 1-2 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#6oceb&quot;)"/></g><g><path fill="none" stroke="#fff" stroke-miterlimit="20" d="M1.727 8v0s2.093 1 4 1c1.906 0 4.062-2 6-2 1.937 0 4 1 4 1v0"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>PICS</span>\
						</a>\
						<a href="#" class="btn-light btn-media-type btn-video-type">\
							<svg style="top:4px;max-width: 20px;max-height: 20px;" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="20" height="20" viewBox="-1 -3 20 20"><defs><clipPath id="dhv7a"><path fill="#fff" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z"/></clipPath></defs><g><g><path fill="none" class="v-ic-0" stroke="#a3b7d6" stroke-miterlimit="20" stroke-width="2" d="M.727 7.5a7.5 7.5 0 1 1 15 0 7.5 7.5 0 0 1-15 0z" clip-path="url(&quot;#dhv7a&quot;)"/></g><g><path class="v-ic" fill="#a3b7d6" d="M10.721 7.5l-3.99 2.508V4.992z"/></g></g></svg>\
							<span>VIDEO</span>\
						</a>\
					</div>\
					<div class="nfcf-carousel-nav custom-arrows">\
						<div class="media-img-nav"></div>\
						<div class="media-video-nav"></div>\
					</div>\
				</div>\
			</div>\
		</div>'
			return WBSlider
		}

		function createImgSlider(e) {
			var imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-images-wrap"><div class="nfcf-images">';
			var imgSlides = '';
			e.forEach(function (elem) {
				imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-img-slide">\
				<div class="img" style="background-image:url('+ elem + ')"></div>\
			</div>'
			})
			imagesSlider += imgSlides;
			imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
			return imagesSlider;
		}
		function createVideoSlider(e) {
			var imagesSlider = '<div class="nfcf-video-wrap"><div class="nfcf-videos">';
			var imgSlides = '';
			e.forEach(function (elem) {
				imgSlides += '<div class="nfcf-vid-slide">\
				<video controls="controls">\
          <source src="'+ elem + '" type="video/mp4">\
          Your browser does not support the video tag.\
        </video>\
			</div>'
			})
			imagesSlider += imgSlides;
			imagesSlider += '</div></div>';
			return imagesSlider;
		}

		//init exercises slider
		function initSlider(parent) {
			$(parent + ' .nfcf-images').slick({
				fade: true,
				dots: true,
				infinite: false,
				prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
				nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
				appendArrows: parent + ' .media-img-nav',
				appendDots: parent + ' .media-img-nav'
			});
			$(parent + ' .nfcf-videos').slick({
				fade: true,
				dots: true,
				infinite: false,
				prevArrow: '<button class="slick-prev">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#f9ac17" d="M6.486 13.605l.003-.004c.268.245.62.398 1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 0 9 12.5c0-.437-.19-.828-.49-1.101l.004-.004L3.72 7l4.794-4.395-.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 0 9 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 7.5 0c-.39 0-.743.153-1.01.398L6.485.394l-6 5.5a1.501 1.501 0 0 0 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
				nextArrow: '<button class="slick-next">button<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="9" height="14" viewBox="0 0 9 14"><g><g><path fill="#eeb00a" d="M2.514 13.605l-.003-.004c-.268.245-.62.398-1.011.398A1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 12.5c0-.437.19-.828.49-1.101l-.004-.004L5.28 7 .486 2.605l.003-.004A1.49 1.49 0 0 1 0 1.5 1.5 1.5 0 0 1 1.5 0c.39 0 .743.153 1.01.398l.004-.004 6 5.5a1.5 1.5 0 0 1 0 2.211z"/></g></g></svg></button>',
				appendArrows: parent + ' .media-video-nav',
				appendDots: parent + ' .media-video-nav'
			}).on('beforeChange', function (e, slick, currentIndex) {
				var currentSlide = jQuery(slick.$slides[currentIndex]);
				var currVideo = currentSlide.find('video');
				if (currVideo.length) {
					currVideo[0].pause();
				}
			})
		}

		//destroy exercises slider
		function destroySlider(parent) {
			$(parent + ' .nfcf-images').slick('unslick');
			$(parent + ' .nfcf-videos').slick('unslick');
		}

		function clearExModalDescr() {
			if (mobileSlider) {
				destroySlider(mobileSlider)
			}
			exModalContent.html('');
		}

		function clearExDesktopDescr() {
			if (desktopSlider) {
				destroySlider(desktopSlider);
				desktopSlider = false;
			}
			exDesktopContent.html('');
		}

		function initML(data) {
			clearExDesktopDescr();
			clearExModalDescr();
			mlComplex.html(createExercises(data));
			enableModal = true;
			setTimeout(function () {
				id = 0;
				$('.exercise').first().trigger('click');
			})
		};

		function showMLComplex() {
			// ajax not working in static markup, needed at least webserver which you can run via grunt
			// 'post' not working without server, so we made 'get'
			// replace type 'get' to 'post' and uncomment 'data'
			data = mlForm.serialize();
			jQuery.ajax({
				url: mlForm.attr('action'),
				type: 'post',
				//type: 'get',
				data: data,
				dataType: 'json',
				success: function (res) {
					nfML.exercisesData = res;
					initML(res);
				},
				error: function (res) {
					console.log('error');
					console.log(res);
				},
				complete: function () {

				}
			});
		}

		function createExercises(data) {
			var exs = '<div class="ml-exercises-wrap"><div class="ml-cat">' + data.category + '</div>';
			data.exercises.forEach(function (e, i) {
				exs += '<a class="exercise" href="' + i + '">' + e.name + '</a>'
			});
			return exs;
		}

		////show exercise descr event
		mlComplex.on('click', '.exercise', function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			$('.exercise.active').removeClass('active');
			$(this).addClass('active');
			id = +$(this).attr('href');
			var descr = createExDescr(nfML.exercisesData.exercises[id]);
			clearExDesktopDescr();
			updateDesktopDescr(descr);
			if (!enableModal) {

				if (window.innerWidth < 768) {
					clearExModalDescr();
					updateMobileDescr(descr);
					if (!enableOpen) {
						$.fancybox.open({
							src: '#ex-info-modal',
							type: 'inline',
							opts: {
								afterClose: function () {
									enableOpen = false;
									enableModal = false;
								}
							}
						}, {
							video: {
								autoStart: false
							}
						});
					}
					enableOpen = false;
				}
			}
			enableModal = false;
		});

		//change video type
		$('body').on('click', '.btn-media-type', function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			if (!$(this).hasClass('active')) {
				var p = $(this).parents('div.nf-exercise-slider-wrap');
				p.toggleClass('nf-show-video');
				p.find('a.btn-media-type').toggleClass('active');
				if ($(this).hasClass('btn-img-type')) {
					p.find('video').each(function () {
						this.pause()
					})
				}
			}
		});
		$('body').on('keyup', '.btn-media-type, .slick-arrow', function (e) {
			var code = (e.keyCode ? e.keyCode : e.which);
			if (code == 13) { //Enter keycode
				$(this).click();
			}
		});

		// open form fields on mobile =============================
		$('.open-form-fields').click(function (e) {
			e.preventDefault();
			$.fancybox.open({
				src: '#ml-fields',
				type: 'inline',
				opts: {
					afterClose: function () {
					}
				}
			}, {
				baseClass: 'vt-fancy-form',
			});
		});

		//clear vmb data
		$('.clear-ml-data').click(function () {
			localStorage.removeItem('mlState20220802');
			location.reload();
		});

		//save nfWB to localStorage
		var updateLocalStoreML = function () {
			try {
				localStorage.setItem('mlState20220802', JSON.stringify(nfML));
			} catch (e) {
				if (e == QUOTA_EXCEEDED_ERR) {
					console.log('Limit is exceeded');
				}
			}
		}
	}

}()

        if(img.attr('src')=='https://servedbyadbutler.com/error/blank.gif' || img.attr('src')=='https://ads.navymwr.org/error/blank.gif'){
